assistant: ```java
public class DivArrayByTwo {
    //@  requires arr == null;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  assigns arr[*];
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == \old(arr[i]) / 2;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static void DivArrayByTwo(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null)
            return;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] == \old(arr[j]) / 2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k; i <= k < arr.length; arr[k] == \old(arr[k]);
        //@ loop_writes arr[*], i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; ++i) {
            arr[i] = arr[i] / 2;
        }
    }
}

user: ```java
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```
assistant: ```java
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class Find {
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length;
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  ensures arr[\result] == elem;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr == null || \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ pure
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;

        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;

public class Max {
    public static int max(int[] a) {

            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int res = a[0];

        for (int i = 1; i < a.length; i++) {
            res = ((a[i] > res) ? a[i] : res);
        }
        return res;
    }
}

public class Max {
    /*@ spec_public @*/
    //@ requires a != null;
    //@ requires a.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] <= \result;
    //@ also
    //@ ensures \result == (\max int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i]);
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] <= \result) && (\exists int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] == \result);
    public static int max(int[] a) {

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= a.length;

        //@ loop_writes res, i;

public class DivArrayByTwo {
    //@  requires arr == null;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  assigns arr[*];
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == \old(arr[i]) / 2;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static void DivArrayByTwo(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null)
            return;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] == \old(arr[j]) / 2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k; i <= k < arr.length; arr[k] == \old(arr[k]);
        //@ loop_writes arr[*], i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }

public class Max {
    //@ requires a != null;
    //@ requires a.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] <= \result;
 
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 < i < a.length; a[i] < \result) && (\exists int i; 0 < i <= a.length; a[i] == \result);  
    public static int max(int[] a) {
        if (a == null || a.length == 0)
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int res = a[0];

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= a.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; a[j] <= res;
        //@ loop_writes res, i;
        //@ decreases a.length - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < a.length; i++) {
            res = ((a[i] > res) ? a[i] : res);
        }
        return res;
    }
}

public class Max {
    //@ requires a != null;
    //@ requires a.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] <= \result;
 
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 < i < a.length; a[i] - 1 <= \result) && (\exists int i; 0 <= i <= a.length; a[i] == \result);  
    public static int max(int[] a) {
        if (a == null || a.length == 0)
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int res = a[0];

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= a.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; a[j] <= res;
        //@ loop_writes res, i;
        //@ decreases a.length - i;

    //@ requires a.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] <= \result;
 
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i <= a.length; a[i] < \result) && (\exists int i; 0 - 1 <= i < a.length; a[i] == \result);  
    public static int max(int[] a) {
        if (a == null || a.length == 0)
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int res = a[0];

    //@ requires a.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] <= \result;
 
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 < i < a.length; a[i] < \result) && (\forall int i; 0 < i <= a.length; a[i] == \result);  
    public static int max(int[] a) {
        if (a == null || a.length == 0)
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int res = a[0];

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= a.length;

    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 < i < a.length; a[i] <= \result) && (\forall int i; 0 <= i <= a.length; a[i] == \result);  
    public static int max(int[] a) {
        if (a == null || a.length == 0)
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int res = a[0];

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= a.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; a[j] <= res;
        //@ loop_writes res, i;
        //@ decreases a.length - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < a.length; i++) {

    //@ requires a.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] <= \result;
 
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 < i <= a.length; a[i] <= \result) && (\exists int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] == \result);  
    public static int max(int[] a) {
        if (a == null || a.length == 0)
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int res = a[0];

    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 < i <= a.length; a[i] - 1 <= \result) && (\exists int i; 0 - 1 <= i < a.length; a[i] == \result);  
    public static int max(int[] a) {
        if (a == null || a.length == 0)
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int res = a[0];

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= a.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; a[j] <= res;
        //@ loop_writes res, i;

public class Max {
    //@ requires a != null;
    //@ requires a.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] <= \result;
 
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] <= \result) && (\forall int i; 0 <= i <= a.length; a[i] == \result);  
    public static int max(int[] a) {
        if (a == null || a.length == 0)
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int res = a[0];

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= a.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; a[j] <= res;
        //@ loop_writes res, i;
        //@ decreases a.length - i;

        return res;

    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] <= \result;
 
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] - 1 <= \result) && (\forall int i; 0 - 1 <= i <= a.length; a[i] == \result);  
    public static int max(int[] a) {
        if (a == null || a.length == 0)
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int res = a[0];

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= a.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; a[j] <= res;
        //@ loop_writes res, i;
        //@ decreases a.length - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < a.length; i++) {

public class Max {
    //@ requires a != null;
    //@ requires a.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] <= \result;
 
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 <= i <= a.length; a[i] - 1 <= \result) && (\exists int i; 0 - 1 <= i < a.length; a[i] == \result);  
    public static int max(int[] a) {
        if (a == null || a.length == 0)
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int res = a[0];

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= a.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; a[j] <= res;
        //@ loop_writes res, i;
        //@ decreases a.length - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < a.length; i++) {

        }
        return res;

    //@ requires a.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;

    public static int max(int[] a) {
        if (a == null || a.length == 0)
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;

    //@ requires a.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] <= \result;
 
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 - 1 <= i <= a.length; a[i] - 1 <= \result) && (\exists int i; 0 <= i <= a.length; a[i] == \result);  
    public static int max(int[] a) {
        if (a == null || a.length == 0)
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int res = a[0];

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= a.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; a[j] <= res;
        //@ loop_writes res, i;
        //@ decreases a.length - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < a.length; i++) {
            res = ((a[i] > res) ? a[i] : res);
        }
        return res;
    }
}

public class Max {
    //@ requires a != null;
    //@ requires a.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] <= \result;
 
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 < i <= a.length; a[i] - 1 <= \result) && (\forall int i; 0 - 1 <= i < a.length; a[i] == \result);  
    public static int max(int[] a) {
        if (a == null || a.length == 0)
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int res = a[0];

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= a.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; a[j] <= res;
        //@ loop_writes res, i;
        //@ decreases a.length - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < a.length; i++) {
            res = ((a[i] > res) ? a[i] : res);
        }
        return res;
    }

public class Max {
    //@ requires a != null;
    //@ requires a.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; a[i] <= \result;
 
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 - 1 <= i <= a.length; a[i] < \result) && (\forall int i; 0 < i <= a.length; a[i] == \result);  
    public static int max(int[] a) {
        if (a == null || a.length == 0)
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int res = a[0];

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= a.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; a[j] <= res;
        //@ loop_writes res, i;
        //@ decreases a.length - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < a.length; i++) {
            res = ((a[i] > res) ? a[i] : res);
        }
        return res;
    }
}
